where the last step holds due to the assumptions (
3.13a
)
and (
3.13b
).

We now turn attention to the estimation error. Regarding the case
with
n\geq p
, we can combine (
D.11
)
and (
D.21
) to conclude that

\displaystyle\bigg|\Big\|\Big(\lambda_{l}\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}^{2}-c_{l}\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\,r\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}|^{2}n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}n}\bigg((\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\log^{2}n}{\sqrt{p\wedge n}}+(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{r\log n}{n}}\bigg).

As for the case with
n<p
, substituting (
D.11
)
and (
D.23
) into (
D.9
) yields

\displaystyle\bigg|\Big\|\Big(\lambda_{l}\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\Big\|_{2}^{2}-c_{l}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})r\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|^{2}n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}pr\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|n^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})r\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|^{2}n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}n},

where in the last line we use the conditions
\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}

and
\sigma^{2}p/n\ll\lambda_{l}^{\star}
(according to the noise
assumption (
3.13a
)).

D.3.1
Proof of Lemma
15

To begin with, let us consider (
D.13
).
By construction, we have
\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}=(\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp})^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{S}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}
,
and it follows from Lemma
21
that
\lambda_{i+1}\leq\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\leq\lambda_{i}

for all
1\leq i<p
. Simple calculation yields

\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}=\frac{(\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\lambda_{i})(\lambda_{l}^{2}-\lambda_{i}\gamma_{i}^{(l)})}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}},

and consequently

\frac{\lambda_{i+1}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i+1})^{2}}\leq\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\leq\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}},\qquad i\geq r.

We can then invoke a similar argument used in the proof of Lemma
14

to bound

\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}\leq\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\leq\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{r}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{r}^{(l)})^{2}}.

Hence, the conclusion immediately follows since
\gamma_{r}^{(l)}/(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{r}^{(l)})^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}(1+p/n)/\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}

by Lemma
8
.

We proceed to consider (
D.14
). According
to Lemma
8
, the following holds
for eigenvalues
\{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\}_{i\geq r}
: (i)
|\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}|\gtrsim\lambda_{l}^{\star}

for all
i\geq r
; (ii)
|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}|\lesssim\sigma^{2}(p\vee n)/n

for all
r\leq i\leq n\wedge(p-1)
; (iii)
\gamma_{i}^{(l)}=0
for

n\wedge(p-1)<i<p
. Therefore, we can upper bound

\bigg|\sum_{i:\,i\geq r}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\bigg|\lesssim(p\wedge n)\frac{\sigma^{2}(p\vee n)}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}n}=\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}},

and the upper bound for
\sum_{i>r}\lambda_{i}/(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}

immediately follows from the triangle inequality.

D.3.2
Proof of Lemma
16

By the definition of
\beta(\cdot)
in (
5.45
),
we can express

\displaystyle\beta(\lambda_{l})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{1\leq i\leq p-r}\frac{\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}.

From Lemma
21
, we know that
\lambda_{i+r}\leq\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})\leq\lambda_{i}

for each
1\leq i\leq p-r
, and thus

\frac{\lambda_{i+r}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i+r}}\leq\frac{\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}\leq\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}},\qquad i>r.

Hence, we have

0\leq\beta(\lambda_{l})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{1\leq i\leq r}\frac{\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}.

(D.24)

As shown in ((
5.44
)), the
eigenvalue
\lambda_{l}
satisfies
\lambda_{l}/\big(1+\beta(\lambda_{l})\big)=\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}+O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})

where
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}
is defined in (
5.59
).
In particular, we note that for the case with
n<p
, the assumption
(
3.13a
) guarantees that

\sigma^{2}=o(\lambda_{\min}^{\star})\qquad\text{and}\qquad\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}\coloneqq(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log n=o(\lambda_{\min}^{\star}).

(D.25)

Combined with (
5.61
), this also implies that

\lambda_{l}\asymp\lambda_{l}^{\star}.

(D.26)

With these estimates in place, one can use (
D.24
)
and the high-probability bound
\|\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top}\|\lesssim\sigma^{2}(1+p/n)\ll\lambda_{l}^{\star}

in (
D.4
) to derive

\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\beta(\lambda_{l})\bigg|\leq\frac{r}{n}\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq r}\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}(\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{\perp}\bm{S}_{\perp}^{\top})}\bigg|\lesssim\frac{r}{n}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\bigg(1+\frac{p}{n}\bigg)=o\Big(\frac{r}{n}\Big)

(D.27)

where the last step holds due the noise condition (
3.13a
).
Meanwhile, we can combine (
5.61
) with (
D.27
)
to find

\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}\bigg|\ll 1

(D.28)

as long as
n\gg r
. Plugging this into ((
5.44
))
reveals that

\displaystyle\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\beta(\lambda_{l})}+O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{=}\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}+o(\frac{r}{n})}+O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}+o\Big(\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}r}{n}\Big)+O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}+O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}),

(D.29)

where (i) holds due to (
D.27
); (ii) follows from
(
D.26
) and (
D.28
);
(iii) holds as long as
r\ll n
. This completes the proof for (
D.15
).

In addition, the claim (
D.16
) is an
immediate consequence of (
D.25
) and (
D.28
).

D.3.3
Proof of Lemma
17

In view of (
5.73
) and the fact
that
\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\overset{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})

(see (
D.8
)), we are motivated to first
decompose

\displaystyle\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}_{=:\,\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}_{=:\,\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\big\{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big\}}_{=:\,\alpha_{3}}.

(D.30)

In what follows, we shall control
\alpha_{1}
,
\alpha_{2}
and

\alpha_{3}
separately in a reverse order.

Controlling
\alpha_{3}
.

We intend to apply Lemma
20
in Section
G

to control
\alpha_{3}
. Before proceeding, we pause to make a few
observations. It is straightforward to compute that
f^{\prime}(x)=\frac{k}{(\lambda-x)^{k+1}}

for the function
f(x):=\frac{1}{(\lambda-x)^{k}}
and
g^{\prime}(x)=\frac{\lambda+(k-1)x}{(\lambda-x)^{k+1}}

for the function
g(x):=\frac{x}{(\lambda-x)^{k}}
. Since
|f(x)-f(y)|\leq\left\{\sup_{z}|f^{\prime}(z)|\right\}|x-y|

for any function
f(\cdot)
, one can demonstrate that: for all
\lambda

satisfying
\lambda/\big(1+\beta(\lambda)\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,
we claim that the following holds

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{1}{\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{\max_{\gamma:\,|\gamma-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}}\frac{1}{(\lambda-\gamma)^{2}}\right\}\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}p/n|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}},

(D.31a)

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{1}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{\max_{\gamma:\,|\gamma-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}}\frac{1}{(\lambda-\gamma)^{3}}\right\}\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}p/n|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 3}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}},

(D.31b)

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{\max_{\gamma:\,|\gamma-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}}\frac{|\lambda+\gamma|}{|\lambda-\gamma|^{3}}\right\}\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\big|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}p/n\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 3}}\cdot\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}},

(D.31c)

and

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{3}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{3}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{\max_{\gamma:\,|\gamma-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}}\frac{|\lambda+2\gamma|}{(\lambda-\gamma)^{4}}\right\}\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\big|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}p/n\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 4}}\cdot\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 3}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}.

(D.31d)

To justify the inequalities above, we have taken
advantage of the following conditions:

•

It is seen from (
5.73
) in Lemma

8
that
|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}
.

•

We have used the condition that for any
\lambda,\gamma
satisfying

\lambda/\big(1+\beta(\lambda)\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})

and
|\gamma-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}
, one has

|\lambda-\gamma|\gtrsim\lambda_{l}^{\star};

this can be established via almost the same argument for justifying
(
5.74
) in Lemma
8

(which we omit here for brevity).

With the preceding upper bounds in place, one can begin to verify
the conditions required to invoke Lemma
20
. First,
one can derive: with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
, for all

\lambda
satisfying
\lambda/\big(1+\beta(\lambda)\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}\lambda}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\big((\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad=\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{3}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{3}}\Big)\big((\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq n\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{3}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{3}}\bigg|\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\big|(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{}{\lesssim}n\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 3}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\cdot(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})^{2}\log n\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{pn}\log n}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}},

where the last line holds due to the upper bound (
D.31c
),
the fact
\sigma^{2}\leq\sigma^{2}p/n\ll\lambda_{l}^{\star}
(from
the noise assumption (
3.13a
)), as well
as the the standard Gaussian concentration inequality. In addition,
one can then apply the matrix Bernstein inequality
(
Koltchinskii 2011
, Corollary 2.1)

to conclude: for any fixed
\lambda
satisfying
\lambda/\big(1+\beta(\lambda)\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,
with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\big((\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\bigg|\cdot(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\cdot(\log^{2}n+\sqrt{n\log n})

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\cdot\lambda_{l}^{\star}(\log^{2}n+\sqrt{n\log n})\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p\log n}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}},

where the second line arises from the the matrix Bernstein inequality,
and the last line follows from (
D.31d
)
and the facts
\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\asymp\lambda_{l}^{\star}

(given the assumption that
\sigma^{2}\leq\sigma^{2}p/n\ll\lambda_{l}^{\star}
).
Taking this together with the fact

\big\{\lambda\colon\lambda/\big(1+\beta(\lambda)\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big\}\subseteq[2\lambda_{l}^{\star}/3,\,4\lambda_{l}^{\star}/3],

we can apply Lemma
20
to show that

|\alpha_{3}|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\sqrt{p\log n}

(D.32)

with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
.

Controlling
\alpha_{2}
.

With regards to
\alpha_{2}
, we claim that the following upper bound
holds, whose proof is deferred to the end of this section.

\left|\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)-\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\right|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}.

(D.33)

Combing this with
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}
defined in (
5.59
),
we arrive at

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{=}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}+O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}

\displaystyle\quad+n\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\bigg|\cdot O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\Bigg(\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\Bigg)\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}

\displaystyle\quad+n\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\bigg|\cdot O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\cdot\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}+n\cdot\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\bigg|\cdot O(\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}})

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{=}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\cdot\lambda_{l}^{\star}\bigg)+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\cdot n\cdot(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{r\log n}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iv})}{=}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}+O\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}\Big).

(D.34)

Here, (i) arises from (
D.15
); (ii) is due
to the claim (
D.33
); (iii) follows from
(
D.16
), (
D.31c
)
and the definition of
\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}
; (iv) holds true
under the condition
\sigma^{2}\ll\lambda_{\max}^{\star}
(see (
D.25
)).

Controlling
\alpha_{1}
.

Regarding
\alpha_{1}
, the key step lies in controlling
\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}
.
Recall that
\bm{U}^{(l)}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(l)}}\bm{V}^{(l)\top}

is the SVD of
\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}
with
\bm{V}^{(l)}\coloneqq[\bm{v}_{1}^{(l)},\cdots,\bm{v}_{n}^{(l)}]\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
.
Towards this, we invoke Theorem
5
to derive
the following identity:

\displaystyle\lambda_{l}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{=}\frac{1}{n}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{S}\bm{S}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\frac{1}{n}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{S}\bm{S}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\Big(\lambda_{l}\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}(\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp})^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{S}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}(\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp})^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{S}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\frac{1}{n}\|\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big(\lambda_{l}\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{=}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{1\leq i\leq n}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{1\leq i\leq n}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{1\leq i\leq n}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{l}}{n}\sum_{1\leq i\leq n}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}},

where (i) arises from (
5.4b
); (ii) relies on the
definitions of
\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}
and
\bm{S}_{l,\perp}
in
(
D.15
); (iii) follows since
\{\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)}\}_{1\leq i\leq n}

forms a set of orthonormal bases in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
. Rearranging
terms, we are left with

\displaystyle n

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq i\leq n}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}=\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}+\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}+\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}+\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}.

As a result, we obtain the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}

\displaystyle=n-\underbrace{\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}}_{=:\,\varphi_{1}}-\underbrace{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)}_{=:\,\varphi_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-\underbrace{\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\big((\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big)}_{=:\,\varphi_{3}}.

(D.35)

In what follows, we shall control
\varphi_{1}
,
\varphi_{2}
and

\varphi_{3}
separately.

•

We start with
\varphi_{1}
. Given that
\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\overset{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,
we can develop an upper bound as follows: with probability at least

1-O(n^{-10})
,

|\varphi_{1}|\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\frac{\sum_{1\leq i<r}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\min_{i:i\neq l}|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}|}\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\lesssim}\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})r\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}|}\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\asymp}\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}r\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}|}.

(D.36)

Here, (i) utilizes the condition
|\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}|\gtrsim\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|

(in view of Lemma
8
), (ii)
holds due to (
D.10
), whereas (iii) arises from the
noise assumption
\sigma^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{l}^{\star}
.

•

As for
\varphi_{2}
, recall from Lemma
21

that
\lambda_{i+1}\leq\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\leq\lambda_{i}
for all
1\leq i<p
.
This in turn leads to

\displaystyle\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)-\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{n}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n};

\displaystyle\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)-\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{r}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}.

This taken collectively with (
D.31a
) yields

\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)-\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\Bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}.

(D.37)

In addition, it is also seen from (
D.31a
)
that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\Bigg|\vee\Bigg|\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\Bigg|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{pn}.

(D.38)

Therefore, we can obtain

\displaystyle\Bigg|(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)-\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\Bigg|(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})-\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\Bigg|\cdot\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\cdot\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)-\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}\sqrt{\frac{r\log n}{n}}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{pn}+\lambda_{l}^{\star}\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n},

where the last step uses (
D.15
), (
D.16
),
(
D.37
) and (
D.38
). This reveals
that

\varphi_{2}=\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq p}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)+O\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}\Big).

(D.39)

•

Turning to
\varphi_{3}
, we shall apply Lemma
20

to bound it. Similar to the analysis above for bounding
\alpha_{3}
,
one can check that the following holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
:
for all
\lambda
satisfying
\lambda/\big(1+\beta(\lambda)\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}\lambda}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\big((\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad=\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\big((\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim n\cdot\max_{r\leq i\leq n}\Bigg|\frac{1}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Bigg|\cdot\max_{r\leq i\leq n}\big|(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim n\cdot\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 3}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\cdot(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\log n\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{pn}\log n,

where the last line comes from (
D.31b
).
In addition, for any fixed
\lambda
such that
\lambda/\big(1+\beta(\lambda)\big)\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,
we can use the matrix Bernstein inequality
(
Koltchinskii 2011
, Corollary 2.1)

to demonstrate that: with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\big((\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\max_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big|\frac{1}{\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}-\frac{1}{\lambda-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big|\cdot(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\log^{2}n+\sqrt{n\log n})

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\sqrt{p\log n},

where the last line comes from (
D.31a
). With
these in place, we invoke Lemma
20
to conclude
that

|\varphi_{3}|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\sqrt{p\log n}

(D.40)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
.

•

Substituting (
D.36
), (
D.39
) and (
D.40
)
into (
D.35
) reveals that: with probability
exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}

\displaystyle=n-\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}+\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}r\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|}\Big).

(D.41)

As a consequence, we arrive at

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\bigg(n-\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+o\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}\Big)+O\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}pr\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|n}\Big),

(D.42)

where we have made use of the bound
\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n\gtrsim\lambda_{l}^{\star}

and
\sigma^{2}p/n=o(\lambda_{l}^{\star}).

Combining the bounds on
\alpha_{1}
,
\alpha_{2}
and
\alpha_{3}
.

Putting (
D.30
), (
D.32
),
(
D.34
) and (
D.42
) together, we conclude

\displaystyle\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{v}_{i}^{(l)\top}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\bigg(n-\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+O\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}pr\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|n}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n}+\frac{\lambda_{l}}{1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i}}}\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+O\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}pr\log n}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|n}\Big)

as claimed.

Proof of the inequality (
D.33
).

Given that
\lambda_{i+1}\leq\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\leq\lambda_{i}
for all

1\leq i<p
, we can bound

\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{n}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{n}^{(l)})^{2}}\leq\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\leq\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{r}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{r}^{(l)})^{2}}.

By subtracting
\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}

from both sides and rearranging terms, we have

\displaystyle\Bigg|\sum_{r\leq i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)-\sum_{r<i\leq n}\Big(\frac{\lambda_{i}}{(\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{i})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Big)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\Bigg|\frac{\gamma_{n}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{n}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Bigg|\vee\Bigg|\frac{\gamma_{r}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{r}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Bigg|.

In view of the basic property
|f(x)-f(y)|\leq\left\{\sup_{z}|f^{\prime}(z)|\right\}|x-y|
,
we can upper bound

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}p/n}{(\lambda_{l}-\sigma^{2}p/n)^{2}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\gamma:\,|\gamma-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}}\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{l}+\gamma}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma)^{3}}\bigg|\cdot\big|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}p/n\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 3}}\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}

for any
r\leq i\leq n
. Here, the last line holds because (i)
|\lambda_{l}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\vee\sigma^{2}(p/n+\sqrt{p/n})\ll\lambda_{l}^{\star}

holds due to the assumption (
3.13a
), and
hence
|\lambda_{l}-\gamma|\gtrsim\lambda_{l}^{\star}
and
\lambda_{l}+\gamma\lesssim\lambda_{l}^{\star}
;
(ii)
|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-\sigma^{2}p/n|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{p/n}

holds according to Lemma
8
.
This finishes the proof for the inequality (
D.33
).

D.4
Proof of Lemma
10

Our proof strategy is to utilize the Gaussian concentration inequality
and the epsilon-net argument.

To apply Lemma
20
, we shall first check its conditions.
To begin with, we claim that the following holds with probability
at least
1-O(n^{-20})
:

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle:=\sup_{\lambda:\frac{\lambda}{1+\beta(\lambda)}\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}\bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\Big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\,\lesssim\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{\left|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\right|}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|}\sqrt{\frac{\left(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\right)(\kappa^{2}+r)}{n}}.

(D.43)

Consequently, we can apply the Gaussian concentration inequality to
show that: for any fixed
\lambda
such that
\lambda/(1+\beta(\lambda))\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,
one has

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\Big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\cdot\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\Big|\lesssim\sqrt{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\log\bigg(\frac{n\kappa\lambda_{\max}}{\Delta_{l}^{\star}}\bigg)}\cdot V

with probability exceeding
1-O\big(\kappa^{-10}(\lambda_{\max}/\Delta_{l}^{\star})^{-20}n^{-20}\big)
.

In addition, one can derive: for all
\lambda
such that
\lambda/(1+\beta(\lambda))\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}\lambda}\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\cdot\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\Big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\cdot\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad=\bigg|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\cdot\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\Big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-2}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\cdot\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{}{\leq}n\cdot\max_{1\leq i<n}\frac{1}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\cdot\bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\bigg\|_{2}\cdot\big\|\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}n\cdot\frac{1}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|^{2}\wedge\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\cdot\sum_{k:k\neq l}|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|\big\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\big\|_{2}\cdot(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{n\log n}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\lesssim}n^{3/2}\cdot\frac{\max_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|}{\min_{i:i\neq l}\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}\right|^{2}\wedge\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}\cdot(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\log n}\cdot\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\lesssim}n^{3/2}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{\star 2}}{\Delta_{l}^{\star 2}}\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}}\cdot V

holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
. Here, (i) uses Lemma

8
and the high-probability
fact that
\|\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}\|_{2}\lesssim(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{n\log n}
,
(ii) holds since
\big\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\big\|_{2}\leq\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\|_{2}\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\perp}\|\leq 1
,
whereas (iii) arises from the definition of
V
in (
D.43
).

Combining the above two bounds, we are ready to invoke Lemma
20

and the union bound to arrive at the advertised bound

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\Big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{s}_{l,\parallel}^{\top}\bigg\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\left(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\right)\log\bigg(\frac{n\kappa\lambda_{\max}}{\Delta_{l}^{\star}}\bigg)}\cdot V

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{\left|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\right|}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\kappa^{2}+r)\log\bigg(\frac{n\kappa\lambda_{\max}}{\Delta_{l}^{\star}}\bigg)}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
.

Therefore, the remainder of the proof amounts to establishing (
D.43
).
Let us work under the event where the claims in Lemma
8

holds, which holds with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-10})
. Recall
the SVD of
\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}=\bm{U}^{(l)}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(l)}}\bm{V}^{(l)\top}
.
Similar to (
C.20
), any

\lambda
such that
\lambda/(1+\beta(\lambda))\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}}(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})
,
one can rewrite

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\Big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\frac{1}{n}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}^{\top}\Big)^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{S}_{l,\perp}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{U}^{(l)}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(l)}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}^{(l)\top}\bm{U}^{(l)}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(l)}}\bm{V}^{(l)\top}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{U}^{(l)}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-1}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(l)}\big)^{-1}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(l)}}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\sqrt{\sum_{1\leq i\leq n\wedge(p-1)}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\bigg(\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\bigg)^{2}}.

•

With regards to the sum over the range
i\geq r
, it is seen from
Lemma
8
and the assumption
(
3.13a
) that for all
i\geq r
,
\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\lesssim\sigma^{2}(1+p/n)\lesssim\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}

and
|\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}|\gtrsim\lambda_{l}^{\star}\geq\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|/\kappa

for any
k\neq i
. This enables us to derive

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{r\leq i\leq n\wedge(p-1)}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\Big(\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\Big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sqrt{(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sum_{r\leq i\leq n\wedge(p-1)}\Big(\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{\kappa}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\Big)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\,\bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{\kappa}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{U}^{(l)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sqrt{(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\kappa^{2}}\,\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{\left|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\right|}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|}\big\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{U}^{(l)}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sqrt{(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\kappa^{2}}\>\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{\left|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\right|}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|},

where the last line holds since
\big\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{U}^{(l)}\big\|_{2}\leq\big\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{(l)}\big\|\leq 1
.

•

Turning to the sum over the range
i<r
, we can control

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\Big(\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\Big)^{2}}

=\Bigg\|\sum_{1\leq i<r}\bigg(\frac{(\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\bigg)\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}
\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{(\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{1/2}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k:k\neq l}|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|\bigg\|\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{(\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{1/2}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}}{\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k:k\neq l}|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|\sqrt{\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}}.

This leads us to control
\sum_{1\leq i\leq r-1}\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)})^{2}/(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}

for each
k\neq l
, which can be decomposed as follows

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}_{1}}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}+\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}_{2}}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}.

Here, the sets
\mathcal{C}_{1}
and
\mathcal{C}_{2}
are defined
respectively as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\{1\leq i<r\mid\gamma_{i}^{(l)}/(1+\gamma(\gamma_{i}^{(l)}))\in\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{k}}(\lambda_{k}^{\star})\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\{1\leq i<r\mid\gamma_{i}^{(l)}/(1+\gamma(\gamma_{i}^{(l)}))\notin\mathcal{B}_{\mathcal{E}_{k}}(\lambda_{k}^{\star})\},

where we take
\mathcal{E}_{k}:=c\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|

for some sufficiently small constant
c>0
. In the sequel, we shall
control the above two sums separately.

–

With respect to the sum over
\mathcal{C}_{1}
, one can apply a similar
argument for (
C.23
) to show
|\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)}|\gtrsim\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|

for
i\in\mathcal{C}_{1}
. This enables us to bound

\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}_{1}}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\big(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\big)^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{C}_{1}}\big(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\big)^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}}\big\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\bm{U}^{(l)}\big\|^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}}.

–

Next, we move on to look at the sum over
\mathcal{C}_{2}
. According
to Lemma
8
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\Big\|\big(\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\bm{I}_{r-1}-(1+\beta(\gamma_{i}^{(l)}))\bm{\Lambda}^{(l)}\big)\bm{U}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i,\parallel}^{(l)}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\big|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-(1+\beta(\gamma_{i}^{(l)}))\lambda_{k}^{(l)}\big|\cdot\big|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i,\parallel}^{(l)}\big|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\mathcal{E}_{k}\cdot\big|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i,\parallel}^{(l)}\big|

\displaystyle\gtrsim|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}|\cdot\big|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\big|,

where we use the fact that
\big|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\big|\leq\big|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i,\parallel}^{(l)}\big|

and
\big|\gamma_{i}^{(l)}-(1+\beta(\gamma_{i}^{(l)}))\lambda_{k}^{(l)}\big|\gtrsim\mathcal{E}_{k}

for all
i\in\mathcal{C}_{2}
. Therefore, we arrive at the upper
bound

\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}\min_{i:i\neq l}|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}|^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}},\qquad i\in\mathcal{C}_{2},

where we invoke the condition
\min_{i:i\neq l}|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{i}^{\star}|\gtrsim\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{PCA}}
.
Taking these two bounds collectively, we reach

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}\big(\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\big)^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}}+\frac{\left(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\right)r}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}}\asymp\frac{\left(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\right)r}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|^{2}},

and hence

\sqrt{\sum_{1\leq i<r}\frac{\gamma_{i}^{(l)}}{(\lambda_{l}-\gamma_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}\Big(\sum_{k:k\neq l}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star(l)\top}\bm{u}_{i}^{(l)}\Big)^{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\left(\lambda_{\max}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\right)r}\sum_{k:k\neq l}\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|}{\left|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}\right|}.

•

Combining the preceding two bounds, we finish the proof for (
D.43
).

Appendix E
Proof for minimax lower bounds (Theorem
4
)

Fix an arbitrary
1\leq l\leq r
and an arbitrary
k\neq l
and

1\leq k\leq r
. In what follows, we intend to prove the following
two claims:

\displaystyle\inf_{u_{\bm{a},l}}\sup_{\bm{\Sigma}\in\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})}\mathbb{E}\Big[\min\big|u_{\bm{a},l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}(\bm{\Sigma})\big|\Big]

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}{|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}|^{2}\,n}\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|+\frac{\sqrt{(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}}{|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}|\sqrt{n}}\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\big|,

(E.1)

\displaystyle\inf_{u_{\bm{a},l}}\sup_{\bm{\Sigma}\in\mathcal{M}_{2}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})}\mathbb{E}\Big[\min\big|u_{\bm{a},l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}(\bm{\Sigma})\big|\Big]

\displaystyle\gtrsim\sqrt{\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}n}}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2},

(E.2)

where the infimum is over all estimators, and
\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})

and
\mathcal{M}_{2}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})
are defined right before
the statement of Theorem
4
.
It is self-evident that Theorem
4

follows from these two claims by taking the maximum over all
k\neq l
.

E.1
Proof of the lower bound (
E.1
)

Step 1: constructing a collection of hypotheses.

Let
us consider the following two hypotheses:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{0}

\displaystyle\,:\,\bm{s}_{i}\overset{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(\bm{0},\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}),\quad 1\leq i\leq n;

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{k}

\displaystyle\,:\,\bm{s}_{i}\overset{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(\bm{0},\bm{\Sigma}_{k}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}),\quad 1\leq i\leq n.

Here, the covariance matrix
\bm{\Sigma}_{k}
is defined as follows:

\bm{\Sigma}_{k}\coloneqq\lambda_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}+\lambda_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}+\sum_{i:\,i\neq k,l,\,1\leq i\leq r}\lambda_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}.

where
\bm{u}_{l}
and
\bm{u}_{k}
are defined as

[\bm{u}_{l},\,\bm{u}_{k}]\coloneqq[\bm{u}_{l}^{\star},\,\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}]\begin{bmatrix}\cos\theta_{n}&-\sin\theta_{n}\\
\sin\theta_{n}&\cos\theta_{n}\end{bmatrix}

(E.3)

for some
\theta_{n}\in[-\pi/2,\pi/2]
to be specified later. Straightforward
calculation yields

\bm{u}_{l}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}+\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}=\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}+\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}.

This identity further leads to

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{k}-\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}+\lambda_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}-(\lambda_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}+\lambda_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top})

\displaystyle=\lambda_{l}^{\star}(\bm{u}_{l}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}+\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top})+(\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star})\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}-\big(\lambda_{l}^{\star}(\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}+\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top})+(\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star})\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\big)

\displaystyle=(\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star})(\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}).

In addition, it is also seen that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Sigma}_{k}-\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=|\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\cdot\|\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq|\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\cdot\big(\|\bm{u}_{k}(\bm{u}_{k}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star})^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|(\bm{u}_{k}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star})\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\big)

\displaystyle=|\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\cdot\big(\|\bm{u}_{k}\|_{2}\|\bm{u}_{k}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\|_{2}+\|\bm{u}_{k}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\|_{2}\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle=2\,|\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\cdot\|\bm{u}_{k}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{=}2\,|\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\cdot\|-\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}+\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\cos\theta_{n}-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\,|\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\cdot\big(\sin\theta_{n}+2\sin^{2}(\theta_{n}/2)\big)

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}4\,|\lambda_{k}^{\star}-\lambda_{l}^{\star}|\cdot|\theta_{n}|,

(E.4)

where (i) arises from the definition of
\bm{u}_{l}
in (
E.3
);
(ii) holds since
\sin\theta\leq|\theta|
.

In what follows, we denote by
\mathbb{P}^{0}
and
\mathbb{P}^{k}

the distribution of
\bm{S}
under the hypothesis
\mathcal{H}_{0}

and
\mathcal{H}_{k}
, respectively, and let
\mathbb{P}_{i}^{0}

and
\mathbb{P}_{i}^{k}
denote the distribution of
\bm{s}_{i}

(
i
-th column of
\bm{S}
) under
\mathcal{H}_{0}
and
\mathcal{H}_{k}
,
respectively.

Step 2: bounding the KL divergence between hypotheses.

Recall the elementary fact that the KL divergence of multivariate
Gaussians is given by
(
Kullback et al. 1952
)

\mathsf{KL}\big(\mathcal{N}(\bm{0},\bm{\Sigma}_{1})\parallel\mathcal{N}(\bm{0},\bm{\Sigma}_{0})\big)=\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\mathsf{tr}\big(\bm{\Sigma}_{0}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{1}\big)-p+\log\frac{|\bm{\Sigma}_{0}|}{|\bm{\Sigma}_{1}|}\bigg).

Since the KL divergence is additive over independent distributions

(
Tsybakov 2009
)
, one has

\displaystyle\mathsf{KL}\big(\mathbb{P}^{k}\parallel\mathbb{P}^{0}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathsf{KL}\big(\mathbb{P}_{i}^{k}\parallel\mathbb{P}_{i}^{0}\big)=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\mathsf{tr}\big((\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{k}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})\big)-p\big).

(E.5)

This suggests that we need to compute
\mathsf{tr}\big((\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{k}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})\big)
.
By construction in (
E.3
), we know that
\bm{u}_{l}

and
\bm{u}_{k}
span the same subspace as
\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}

and
\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}
, and are orthogonal to
\{\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\}_{i:i\neq k,l}
.
Denote by
\bm{U}^{\star\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times(p-r)}
the matrix
whose columns form an orthonormal basis of the complement to the subspace
spanned by
\bm{U}^{\star}
. One can then derive

\displaystyle(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{k}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{1\leq i\leq r}\frac{1}{\lambda_{i}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\bm{U}^{\star\perp}(\bm{U}^{\star\perp})^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\bigg((\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\bm{u}_{l}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}+(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}+\sum_{i:\,i\neq k,l,\,1\leq i\leq r}(\lambda_{i}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}+\sigma^{2}\bm{U}^{\star\perp}(\bm{U}^{\star\perp})^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}+\frac{1}{\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\Big)\Big((\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\bm{u}_{l}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}+(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i:\,i\neq k,l,\,1\leq i\leq r}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}+\bm{U}^{\star\perp}(\bm{U}^{\star\perp})^{\top}.

As a result, we find

\displaystyle\mathsf{tr}\big((\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{k}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{=}\mathsf{tr}\bigg(\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}+\frac{1}{\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\Big)\Big((\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\bm{u}_{l}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}+(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\Big)\bigg)+p-2

\displaystyle\qquad=|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}|^{2}+\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}+\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}|^{2}+|\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}+p-2

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\cos^{2}\theta_{n}+\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\sin^{2}\theta_{n}+\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\sin^{2}\theta_{n}+\cos^{2}\theta_{n}+p-2

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})^{2}+(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}\sin^{2}\theta_{n}-2\sin^{2}\theta_{n}+p

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star})^{2}}{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}\sin^{2}\theta_{n}+p.

Here, (i) holds since
\mathsf{tr}(\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top})=1

and
\mathsf{tr}\big(\bm{U}^{\star\perp}(\bm{U}^{\star\perp})^{\top}\big)=\mathsf{tr}\big((\bm{U}^{\star\perp})^{\top}\bm{U}^{\star\perp}\big)=\mathsf{tr}(\bm{I}_{p-r})=p-r
;
(ii) follows from the following observations:

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}

\displaystyle=\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\cos\theta_{n}+\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}=\cos\theta_{n};

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}

\displaystyle=\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\cos\theta_{n}+\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}=\sin\theta_{n};

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=-\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}+\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\cos\theta_{n}=-\sin\theta_{n};

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=-\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}+\bm{u}_{k}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\cos\theta_{n}=\cos\theta_{n};

where we have used the construction (
E.3
) and the
fact that
\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}=0
. Therefore,
combining the above identities allows us to conclude that

\mathsf{KL}\big(\mathbb{P}^{k}\parallel\mathbb{P}^{0}\big)=\frac{n(\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star})^{2}}{2(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}\sin^{2}\theta_{n}.

(E.6)

Step 3: invoking Fano’s inequality.

Suppose that we
choose
\theta_{n}

|\theta_{n}|=c_{n}\sqrt{\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star})^{2}n}}

(E.7)

where
c_{n}\asymp 1
is a sequence that depends on
n
and obeys

c_{n}\in\{1/64,\,1/16,\,1/4\}
(which we shall discuss momentarily).
Then we can see from (
E.4
) that

\|\bm{\Sigma}_{k}-\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}{n}}.

In other words,
\bm{\Sigma}_{k}\in\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})
.
Moreover, plugging the value (
E.7
) of

\theta_{n}
into (
E.6
) and using the facts

|\sin\theta|\leq|\theta|
as well as
\max_{n}c_{n}=1/4
yields

\mathrm{\mathsf{KL}}(\mathbb{P}^{k}\parallel\mathbb{P}^{0})\leq 1/16.

It then follows from Fano’s inequality
(
Tsybakov 2009
, Theorem 2)

that

p_{e,k}:=\inf_{\psi}\max\Big\{\mathbb{P}\{\psi\;{\rm rejects}\;\mathcal{H}_{0}\mid\mathcal{H}_{0}\},\,\mathbb{P}\{\psi\;{\rm rejects}\;\mathcal{H}_{k}\mid\mathcal{H}_{k}\}\Big\}\geq 1/5,

where the infimum is taken over all tests. One can then apply the
standard reduction scheme in
(
Tsybakov 2009
, Chapter 2.2)

to show that

\inf_{u_{\bm{a},l}}\sup_{\bm{\Sigma}\in\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})}\mathbb{E}\Big[\min\big|u_{\bm{a},l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}(\bm{\Sigma})\big|\Big]\gtrsim p_{e,k}\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\gtrsim\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|.

Observe that once we prove

\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\geq\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}}\big(\theta_{n}^{2}\cdot\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|+|\theta_{n}|\cdot\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\big|\big),

(E.8)

then (
E.7
) would immediately lead to
the advertised bound

\displaystyle\inf_{u_{\bm{a},l}}\sup_{\bm{\Sigma}\in\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})}\mathbb{E}\Big[\min\big|u_{\bm{a},l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}(\bm{\Sigma})\big|\Big]

\displaystyle\gtrsim c_{n}\frac{(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|}{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star})^{2}n}+c_{n}\frac{\sqrt{(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\big|}{|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}|\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|}{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star})^{2}n}+\frac{\sqrt{(\lambda_{k}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})}\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\big|}{|\lambda_{l}^{\star}-\lambda_{k}^{\star}|\sqrt{n}}

where the last step holds since
\min_{n}c_{n}=1/64
. As a consequence,
the remainder of the proof amounts to establishing the claim (
E.8
).
In view of (
E.3
), we know that

\displaystyle\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\bm{a}^{\top}(\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\cos\theta_{n}+\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n})\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}(1-\cos\theta_{n})-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}\big|

\displaystyle=\big|2\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\sin^{2}(\theta_{n}/2)-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{=}\big|2\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\sin^{2}(\theta_{n}/2)\big|+\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\geq}\frac{2}{\pi^{2}}\big(\theta_{n}^{2}\cdot|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}|+|\theta_{n}|\cdot\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\big|\big)

(E.9)

where (i) holds true as long as we choose
\mathsf{sign}(\theta_{n})=-\mathsf{sign}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}/\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star})
;
(ii) relies on the fact
|\sin\theta|\geq\frac{2}{\pi}|\theta|
for

\theta\in[-\frac{\pi}{2},\frac{\pi}{2}]
. In addition, we can derive

\displaystyle\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\bm{a}^{\top}(\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\cos\theta_{n}+\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n})\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}(1+\cos\theta_{n})+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin\theta_{n}\big|

\displaystyle=\big|2\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\cos^{2}(\theta_{n}/2)+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin(\theta_{n}/2)\cos(\theta_{n}/2)\big|

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta_{n}/2)\big|2\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\cos(\theta_{n}/2)+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin(\theta_{n}/2)\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\geq}\frac{1}{2}\,\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\cos(\theta_{n}/2)+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}\sin(\theta_{n}/2)\big|

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{=}\frac{1}{2}\,\Big|\sqrt{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star})^{2}+(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star})^{2}}\sin(\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k})\Big|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4}\,\big(|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}|\sin|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|\sin|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|\big)

(E.10)

where (i) holds due to
|\theta_{n}|\leq 1/4
by the choice of
c_{n}

in (
E.7
) and the sample size condition
(
3.20
);
\omega_{k}\in[-\frac{\pi}{2},\frac{\pi}{2}]

in (ii) is defined such that
\tan\omega_{k}=\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}/\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}
.
In particular, recall that the sign of
\theta_{n}
is chosen such
that
\mathsf{sign}(\theta_{n})=-\mathsf{sign}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}/\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star})
,
one has
\mathsf{sign}(\theta_{n})=-\mathsf{sign}(\omega_{k})
. Next,
our goal is to show if we choose
c_{n}\in\{1/64,\,1/16,\,1/4\}

of
\theta_{n}
in (
E.7
) suitably,
one has

\sin|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|\geq\frac{1}{2\pi}|\theta_{n}|.

(E.11)

To this end, for each
n
, we choose
c_{n}
of
\theta_{n}
in
(
E.7
) to be
c_{n}=1/16
temporarily,
and consider the following three scenarios:

•

If
|\theta_{n}|/2\geq 2\,|\omega_{k}|
, then one has
\pi/2\geq|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|\geq|\theta_{n}|/2-|\omega_{k}|\geq|\theta_{n}/4|

where the first inequality holds since the signs of
\theta_{n}

and
\omega_{k}
are different. Combined with the inequality
|\sin\theta|\geq\frac{2}{\pi}|\theta|

for
\theta\in[-\frac{\pi}{2},\frac{\pi}{2}]
, this leads to
\sin|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|\geq\sin|\theta_{n}/4|\geq\frac{1}{2\pi}|\theta_{n}|;

•

If
|\theta_{n}|/2|\leq|\omega_{k}|/2
, then we know
\pi/2\geq|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|\geq|\omega_{k}|-|\theta_{n}|/2\geq|\omega_{k}/2|\geq|\theta_{n}/2|
.
This implies that
\sin|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|\geq\sin|\theta_{n}/2|\geq\frac{1}{\pi}|\theta_{n}|
.

•

Otherwise, (i.e.
|\omega_{k}|/2<|\theta_{n}|/2<2\,|\omega_{k}|
),
one can adjust
c_{n}
to be either
1/4
or
1/64
(namely, increasing
it or decreasing it by
4
times). After doing so, it is easily seen
that
\theta_{n}
must satisfy one of the two conditions above, thereby
guaranteeing that
\sin|\theta_{n}/2+\omega_{k}|\geq\frac{1}{2\pi}|\theta_{n}|
.

This completes the proof for the claim (
E.11
).
Combining (
E.11
) with (
E.10
),
we arrive at

\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}\big|\geq\frac{1}{8\pi}\big(|\theta_{n}|\cdot|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}|+|\theta_{n}|\cdot|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|\big)\geq\frac{1}{8\pi^{2}}\big(\theta_{n}^{2}\cdot|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}|+|\theta_{n}|\cdot|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}^{\star}|\big),

where the last step holds since
|\theta_{n}|\leq 1
. Combining this
with (
E.9
) finishes the proof of the claim
(
E.8
).

E.2
Proof of the lower bound (
E.2
)

Step 1: constructing a collection of hypotheses.

Consider
the following hypotheses regarding the eigen-decomposition of the
covariance matrix:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{0}

\displaystyle\,:\,\bm{s}_{i}\overset{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(\bm{0},\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}),\quad 1\leq i\leq n;

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1}

\displaystyle\,:\,\bm{s}_{i}\overset{\mathrm{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(\bm{0},\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}),\quad 1\leq i\leq n.

Here, the covariance matrix
\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}
is defined
to be

\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}\coloneqq\lambda_{l}^{\star}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}+\sum_{i:i\neq l}\lambda_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top},

where
\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}
is defined as

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}_{\perp}\|_{2}}=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\qquad\text{with}\quad\bm{a}_{\perp}:=\frac{\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}}{\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}}

for some
0<\delta_{n}<1
to be specified later. We note that
\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}=0

for all
1\leq i\leq r
and
\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}=0

for all
i\neq l
. As can be straightforwardly verified, one has

\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}-\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\|_{2}\leq\Big(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\Big)\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\|_{2}+\frac{\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\|\bm{a}_{\perp}\|_{2}=\frac{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}-1+\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\leq 2\delta_{n},

(E.12)

where the last step holds since
\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}\leq 1+\delta_{n}

for
\delta_{n}>0
.

In the sequel, we denote by
\mathbb{P}^{0}
and
\mathbb{P}^{1}

the distribution of
\bm{S}
under the hypothesis
\mathcal{H}_{0}

and
\mathcal{H}_{1}
, respectively. We also let
\mathbb{P}_{i}^{0}

and
\mathbb{P}_{i}^{1}
denote the distribution of
\bm{s}_{i}

(
i
-th column of
\bm{S}
) under
\mathcal{H}_{0}
and
\mathcal{H}_{1}
,
respectively.

Step 2: bounding the KL divergence between hypotheses.

Let
us define vector
\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}
as

\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}:=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\frac{1}{\delta_{n}}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\frac{1}{\delta_{n}}\bm{a}_{\perp}\|_{2}}=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\frac{1}{\delta_{n}}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{1+\frac{1}{\delta_{n}^{2}}}}.

where the last step holds since
\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}
is orthogonal
to
\bm{a}_{\perp}
. Note that
\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}
is a unit
vector orthogonal to the subspace spanned by
\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}

and
\{\bm{u}_{i}^{\star}\}_{i\neq l}
, namely,
\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}=0

and
\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}=0
for all
i\neq l
.
Similar to the proof for the claim (
E.1
),
one can derive

\displaystyle\mathsf{tr}\big((\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})^{-1}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})\big)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{tr}\bigg(\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\bm{a}_{\perp}\bm{a}_{\perp}^{\top}\Big)\Big((\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}+\sigma^{2}\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\Big)\bigg)+p-2

\displaystyle=(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star})^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star})^{2}+\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}(\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp})^{2}+(\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp})^{2}+p-2

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}\bigg(1+\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\delta_{n}^{2}\bigg)+\frac{1}{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}}\delta_{n}^{2}+1\bigg)+p-2

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}\frac{\delta_{n}^{2}}{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}+p,

where the second step is due to
\bm{u}_{i}^{\star\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}=0

and the third line follows from the following facts:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}}{\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}_{\perp}\|_{2}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}};

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}_{\perp}\|_{2}}=\frac{\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}};

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\frac{1}{\delta_{n}}\bm{a}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}}{\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\frac{1}{\delta_{n}}\bm{a}_{\perp}\|_{2}}=\frac{\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}};

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{u}}_{l}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{u}_{l}^{\star\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}-\frac{1}{\delta_{n}}\bm{a}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}-\frac{1}{\delta_{n}}\bm{a}_{\perp}\|_{2}}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}.

As a consequence, we can upper bound the KL divergence as follows

\displaystyle\mathsf{KL}\big(\mathbb{P}^{1}\parallel\mathbb{P}^{0}\big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathsf{KL}\big(\mathbb{P}_{i}^{1}\parallel\mathbb{P}_{i}^{0}\big)=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\mathsf{tr}\big((\bm{\Sigma}^{\star}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})^{-1}(\widetilde{\bm{\Sigma}}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p})\big)-p\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{n\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}{2(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}\frac{\delta_{n}^{2}}{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}\leq\frac{\delta_{n}^{2}n\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}}{2(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}.

Step 3: invoking Fano’s inequality.

From the preceding upper bound on the KL divergence, it is easy to
see that
\mathrm{\mathsf{KL}}(\mathbb{P}^{1}\parallel\mathbb{P}^{0})\leq 1/16

if we choose

\delta_{n}=c_{n}\sqrt{\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}n}}\leq 1,

(E.13)

where
c_{n}\asymp 1
obeys
c_{n}\in\{1/64,\,1/16,\,1/4\}
and the
last step holds due to the assumption (
3.20
).
It follows from Fano’s inequality
(
Tsybakov 2009
, Theorem 2)

that

p_{e}:=\inf_{\psi}\max\big\{\mathbb{P}\{\psi\;{\rm rejects}\;\mathcal{H}_{0}\mid\mathcal{H}_{0}\},\,\mathbb{P}\{\psi\;{\rm rejects}\;\mathcal{H}_{1}\mid\mathcal{H}_{1}\}\big\}\geq 1/5,

where the infimum is taken over all tests. Further, we know from (
E.12
),
(
E.13
) and
c_{n}\leq 1/4
that

\|\widetilde{\bm{u}}-\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}n}},

namely,
\bm{\Sigma}_{1}\in\mathcal{M}_{2}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})
.

Next, let us continue to control
\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|
.
Our goal is to show

\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\gtrsim\delta_{n}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2},

and we shall use the same argument as for (
E.8
) to
prove it. Towards this, let us first consider
\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}-\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}\big|
.
By construction, one can derive

\displaystyle\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}=\underbrace{\frac{\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}}_{=:\,\eta_{1}}-\underbrace{\bigg(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\bigg)\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}}_{=:\,\eta_{2}}

(E.14)

where the last step holds because

\displaystyle\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}/\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}=(\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a})^{\top}\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}/\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}=\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}.

Moreover, it is straightforward to verify that

\frac{1}{4}\delta_{n}^{2}\leq 1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\leq\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}-1\leq\frac{1}{2}\delta_{n}^{2}

(E.15)

for
0<\delta_{n}<1
. With these basic facts in place, let us first
choose the pre-factor
c_{n}\asymp 1
in
E.13

to be
c_{n}=1/16
for the moment, and compare the two terms on the
right-hand side of (
E.14
).

•

If
|\eta_{1}|\geq 2\,|\eta_{2}|
, then one has

\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\geq|\eta_{1}|-|\eta_{2}|\geq\frac{|\eta_{1}|}{2}\geq\frac{\delta_{n}}{4}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2},

where we have used the fact that
\delta_{n}\leq 1
.

•

If
|\eta_{1}|\leq\,|\eta_{2}|/2
, then we know that

\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\geq|\eta_{2}|-|\eta_{1}|\geq|\eta_{1}|\geq\frac{\delta_{n}}{2}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}

as long as
\delta_{n}\leq 1
.

•

Otherwise, consider the case where
|\eta_{2}|/2<|\eta_{1}|<2\,|\eta_{2}|
.
In this case, we can adjust the pre-factor
c_{n}
to be
1/4
.
By doing so,
|\eta_{1}|
increases by at most
4
times, while

|\eta_{2}|
increases by at least
8
times (according to (
E.15
)).
As a result, the new values of
\eta_{1}
and
\eta_{2}
satisfy

|\eta_{1}|\leq\,|\eta_{2}|/2
, thus belonging to the second case
discussed above and hence
\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\geq\frac{\delta_{n}}{4}\,\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}.

Clearly, we can also adjust
c_{n}
to be
1/64
so as to meet the
condition of the first case discussed above.

To sum up, the above analysis reveals that: by properly choosing the
constants
\{c_{n}\}
in (
E.13
), one
can guarantee that

\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\geq\frac{\delta_{n}}{4}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}.

Similarly, we can also derive

\displaystyle\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}+\delta_{n}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{a}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bigg|=\bigg|\frac{\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\bigg)\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\bigg|

\displaystyle\geq\bigg|\frac{\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}-\bigg(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\Big)\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\bigg|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{\delta_{n}}{\sqrt{1+\delta_{n}^{2}}}\,\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}

Taking these two relations collectively yields the advertised bound:

\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\gtrsim\delta_{n}\,\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}.

As a consequence, one can readily apply the standard reduction scheme
in
(
Tsybakov 2009
, Chapter 2.2)
again to arrive that

\displaystyle\inf_{u_{\bm{a},l}}\sup_{\bm{\Sigma}\in\mathcal{M}_{2}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\star})}\mathbb{E}\Big[\min\big|u_{\bm{a},l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}(\bm{\Sigma})\big|\Big]

\displaystyle\gtrsim p_{e}\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|\gtrsim\min\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\widetilde{\bm{u}}_{l}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}^{\star}\big|

\displaystyle\gtrsim c_{n}\sqrt{\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}n}}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}\gtrsim\sqrt{\frac{(\lambda_{l}^{\star}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}^{\star 2}n}}\|\bm{P}_{\bm{U}^{\star\perp}}\bm{a}\|_{2}

where the last step holds since
\min_{n}c_{n}=1/64
.

Appendix F
Proof for the lower bound of the plug-in estimator (Theorem
2
)

Evidently, it is sufficient to establish the lower bound for the rank-
1
case (i.e.
r=1
),
which forms the content of this section.
To begin with, let us decompose the leading eigenvector
\bm{u}
of
\bm{M}
as follows:

\displaystyle\bm{u}=\bm{u}^{\star}\cos\theta+\bm{u}_{\perp}\sin\theta,\qquad\text{with }\theta\in\big[0,\pi/2\big],

where as before,
\bm{u}_{\perp}
denotes some unit vector perpendicular to
\bm{u}^{\star}
.
Denote by
s\coloneqq\mathrm{sign}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp})
the sign of
\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}
.
Armed with these, one can express the plug-in estimator
u_{\bm{a}}^{\mathsf{plugin}}
as

\displaystyle u_{\bm{a}}^{\mathsf{plugin}}=\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}=\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\cos\theta+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}\sin\theta=\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\cos\theta+s\,|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|\sin\theta,

which in turn leads to

\displaystyle\mathsf{dist}\left(u_{\bm{a}}^{\mathsf{plugin}},\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=\min\left|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}\pm\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\right|=\left||\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}|-|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}|\right|

\displaystyle=\Big||\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}|-\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\cos\theta+s\,|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|\sin\theta\big|\Big|.

In view of the analysis in Appendix
C.4
, one can see that: conditioned on
\theta
,
\bm{u}_{\perp}
is uniformly distributed over the unit sphere when restricted to the subspace spanned by the columns of
\bm{u}^{\star\perp}
.
Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big\{s=1\,\mid\,|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|,\theta\big\}=\mathbb{P}\big\{s=-1\,\mid\,|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|,\theta\big\}=\frac{1}{2}.

This implies the independence between
s
and
|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|
as well as
\theta
, which further reveals that

\displaystyle s=\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{s^{\prime}=\pm 1}\Big||\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}|-\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\cos\theta+s^{\prime}\,|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|\sin\theta\big|\Big|

with probability
1/2
.
Therefore, it is seen that

\displaystyle\mathsf{dist}\left(u_{\bm{a}}^{\mathsf{plugin}},\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\right)=\max_{s^{\prime}=\pm 1}\Big||\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}|-\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\cos\theta+s^{\prime}\,|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|\sin\theta\big|\Big|

with probability
1/2
.
In the following, we seek to lower bound
\max_{s^{\prime}=\pm 1}\big||\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}|-|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}^{\star}\cos\theta+s^{\prime}\,|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{\perp}|\sin\theta|\big|
,
